NOTICE OF PUBLIC HEARING AND AGENDA
NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS
Tuesday, June 27, 2017
ONE OR MORE SUPERVISORS MAY ATTEND VIA VIDEO AND/OR TELEPHONICALLY 
The Navajo County Board of Supervisors will hold a meeting open to the public on Tuesday, June 27, 2017, in the Supervisors’
Chamber in the Navajo County Governmental Complex, Holbrook, Arizona. The Board may vote to meet in a closed executive
session to discuss certain matters as indicated on the following agenda. The Board may meet in executive session pursuant to
A.R.S. § 38-431.03(A) (3) for legal advice on any item.
Persons with disabilities who need accommodation to attend or participate in the meeting may contact the Clerk of the Board’s office
at (928) 524-4053; at least 48 hours prior to the meeting so an accommodation can be arranged.
Items on the agenda may be considered out of order at the Chairman’s discretion. A copy of the agenda background material
provided to the Board, with the exception of items to be considered in executive session, is available for public inspection at Navajo
County website, www.navajocountyaz.gov 
9:00 a.m. Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation
           
1. CALL TO THE PUBLIC: Individuals may address the Board on any relevant issue for up to 5
minutes. At the close of the call to the public, Board members may not respond to any
comments but may respond to criticism, ask staff to review a matter or ask that a matter be
placed on a future agenda.
 
 
 
2. CONSENT AGENDA: These items are considered to be routine or administrative in nature
and will be approved in a single motion. A Board member may request for any item to be
removed from the consent agenda and considered on the regular agenda:
 
 
a. Minutes: June 13, 2017  
 
b. Constable Reports: Holbrook Precinct #1, May; Snowflake Precinct #3, May; and Show Low
Precinct #5, May 2017 
 
 
c. Special Warranty Deed conveying real property to Timber Mesa Fire and Medical District, a
political subdivision of the State of Arizona.
 
 
d. Approval to conduct Bond Election for the Show Low USD No. 10 on November 7, 2017.  
 
e. Resolution Number 11-17, cancelling the August 29, 2017 Election for the Porter Creek
Domestic Water Improvement District and appoint those individuals whom filed nominating
petitions to fill those vacant positions.
 
 
f. Sale of surplus suitcase style voting booths to Navajo Nation, Hopi Nation and the White
Mountain Apache Tribe for their Tribal Elections for $1.00 each.
 
 
g. Revolving Credit Agreement between Wells Fargo Bank and Navajo County for credit lines for
Navajo County and Navajo County Political Subdivisions.

<!-- page 2 -->
h. Arizona Criminal Justice Commission Byrne grant #DC-18-009 for $234,416 for operation of
the Major Crimes Apprehension Team.
 
 
i. Grant agreement #HT-17-2717 with City of Tucson for High Intensity Drug Trafficking Areas
(HIDTA) funding for $118,877.
 
 
j. Installation of two (2) permanent "25 mph" speed limit signs on FS-504 Road.  
 
k. Navajo County Comments, Definition of Waters of the United States under the Clean Water
Act. 
 
 
l. Resolution Number 12-17, requesting NACo to urge congress to support additional funding
for school bus routes on Indian Reservations. 
 
 
m. Approval of 2 Back Tax Land Parcel sold over the counter in the amount of $1,314.26.  
 
n. Appointment of Melissa Lynn Foy-Jentoft, Laura Vandenheede, Debbie Louise
Houston-Robertson and Lamista Maria Schultz as Democratic Precinct Committeemen for their
respective precincts.
 
 
o. Acceptance of the FY18 Arizona Criminal Justice Commission's Byrne Grant.  
 
3. NAVAJO COUNTY PUBLIC HEALTH SERVICES DISTRICT: Board of Directors
Session: 
 
 
a. CONSENT AGENDA: The following item(s) are considered to be of a routine or
administrative in nature and will be approved as a group unless a member of the Board wishes
to discuss a particular item: 
 
 
1. Contract with Northern Arizona Radiology to provide X-ray services who have or are
reasonably at risk of having tuberculosis.
 
 
4. EMPLOYEE RECOGNITION:  
 
a. Navajo County Recognizes the following employees for their excellence in personal
performance far exceeding organization expectations: Kirsti Hancock (Adult Probation); Leah
Thomas (Superior Court); Lewis Allen (Public Works); and Michael Sample (Clerk of the
Court).
 
Presented By: Lea Petersen
 
5. CONSTABLE:  
 
a. Acceptance of the Resignation of Constable Ryan Reinhold of Justice Precinct #6, effective
June 30, 2017 and possible appointment of Jack Gregory (Greg) Sharp to fill the remainder of
the unexpired term of the vacant office. 
 
Presented By: Constable Ryan Reinhold

<!-- page 3 -->
6. ADMINISTRATIVE SERVICES:  
 
a. Consideration and possible approval of Personnel Actions.
Presented By: Eric Scott
 
 
b. Personnel Service Awards: Congratulations to the following employees for their continued
service to Navajo County: Donna McMonigal (Sheriff) 25 years; Sabrina James (Recorder) 20
years; Cindy Miller (Treasurer) 5 years; Terance Yazzie (Public Works) 5 years; Frank
Reed (Public Works) 5 years; and Robert Schlesinger (BOS/Administration) 5 years.
 
Presented By: Eric Scott
 
c. Presentation to retiring employee Lea Egnor with the Snowflake Justice Court after 28 years of
dedicated service. 
 
Presented By: Eric Scott/Yvonne Hunt
 
d. Consideration of a new Navajo County Social Media Policy.  
Presented By: Adam Wolfe
 
e. Consideration of the sales of the Superintendent of Schools surplus items to be sold in an online
auction to be held in July 2017.
 
Presented By: Joyce Alexander
 
f. PUBLIC HEARING: Consideration of the repeal of the Outdoor Fire Ordinance, Ordinance
Number 01-15, and adoption of a new Outdoor Fire Ordinance, Ordinance Number 01-17. 
 
Presented By: Mary Springer
 
g. PUBLIC HEARING: Consideration of the repeal of the Navajo County Fireworks Ordinance,
Ordinance Number 01-12 and adoption of a new Navajo County Fireworks Ordinance,
Ordinance Number 02-17.
 
Presented By: Mary Springer
 
h. Consideration and adoption of the proposed Tentative Budget for Fiscal Year 2017-18 for Navajo
County.
 
Presented By: Bryan Layton

Superior Courts of the State of Arizona in and for the Counties of Navajo and Pinal County,
and Pinal County, for Use of the Pinal County Youth Justice Center. 
 
Presented By: Bryan Layton
 
7. BOARD OF EQUALIZATION:  
 
a. Discussion and action regarding Petition for Review of Real Property Valuation APN
403-23-015; Swearing in of Assessor, Appraisers and Petitioners. 
 
Presented By: Cammy Darris/Melissa Buckley
 
b. Discussion and action regarding Petition for Review of Real Property Valuation APN
210-23-016U & 210-02-042C; Swearing in of Assessor, Appraisers and Petitioners. 
 
Presented By: Cammy Darris/Melissa Buckley
 
8. PUBLIC WORKS:  
 
a. Consideration and adoption of Resolution Number 13-17, approving the request for
Abandonment of Caliente Drive in Winslow. 
 
Presented By: Bill Bess
 
b. PLANNING & ZONNING:  
 
1. PUBLIC HEARING: Consideration and adoption of Resolution Number 14-17
approving/denying a Final Plat on 7.8 acres, subdividing 7.8 acres into 4 lots on a portion of
APN 409-24-001C a portion of Township 10 North, Range 21 East, Section 8 in the Linden
area.
 
Presented By: Sandy Phillips 
 
2. PUBLIC HEARING: Consideration and adoption of Resolution Number 15-17

<!-- page 5 -->
2. PUBLIC HEARING: Consideration and adoption of Resolution Number 15-17
approving/denying a Special Use Permit to allow a "Glamping" Recreation Camp on APN
404-51-008, a portion of Township 11 North, Range 23 East, Section 1 in the Concho area. 
 
Presented By: Sandy Phillips 
 
3. PUBLIC HEARING: Consideration and adoption of Resolution Number 16-17
approving/denying a Zone Change on 7.8 acres currently zoned A-General to R1-43 on a
portion of APN 409-24-001C a portion of Township 10 North, Range 21 East, Section 8 in the
Linden area. 
 
Presented By: Sandy Phillips 
 
4. PUBLIC HEARING: Consideration and adoption of Resolution Number 17-17
approving/denying a revision to the Navajo County Zoning Ordinance to modify the definition
of the term "Setback" From - "A line which defines the future right-of-way of streets". To:  "The
minimum horizontal distance required between a property line and a structure, or in the event
a dedicated ingress/egress easement is interior to the property line, the setback is the
horizontal distance between the easement line and a structure."
 
Presented By: Sandra Phillips
 
9. BOARD OF SUPERVISORS:  
 
a. Consideration and Acceptance of the Resignation of James Jayne, County Manager effective
August 4, 2017 and Direction to Staff to Develop Criteria and a Job Description to be posted
for the County Manager position. 
 
Presented By: Melissa Buckley
 
b. EXECUTIVE SESSION pursuant to A.R.S. § 38-431.03(A) (4) for the Board to receive legal
advice from its attorney regarding possible action of Notice of Claim submitted by Jake Caley.
 
Presented By: Brandt Clark 
 
c. EXECUTIVE SESSION pursuant to A.R.S. § 38-431.03(A) (4) for the Board to receive legal
advice from its attorney regarding settlement negotiations to resolve the Transwestern Pipeline
litigation.
 
Presented By: Jason Moore
 
d. RECONVENE: Authorization for the Deputy County Attorney and Risk Manager to enter
into settlement negotiations on the Notice of Claim filed by Jake Caley, with an amount not to
exceed the amount discussed in executive session. 
 
 
e. RECONVENE: Motion to authorize the Attorney General's office and ADOR to enter into
settlement negotiations to resolve the Transwestern Pipeline litigation on the terms discussed
with legal counsel during executive session. 
 
 
10. COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE: Report from Board members, County Attorney and
Assistant County Manager.
 
 
 
11. ADJOURN  
 
12. NAVAJO COUNTY HEALTH FAIR: Pursuant to ARS § 38-431.02 and ARS § 38-431 (3),

<!-- page 6 -->
12. NAVAJO COUNTY HEALTH FAIR: Pursuant to ARS § 38-431.02 and ARS § 38-431 (3),
notice is hereby given to the members of the Navajo County Board of Supervisors and to the
General Public that a quorum of the Board of Supervisors may be present for the Navajo
County Health Fair, at 100 East Code Talkers Drive in Holbrook, Arizona where no official
action will be taken.

Board of Supervisors Regular 2.d.        
Meeting Date: 06/27/2017  
Title: COE Show Low USD Nov 2017
Submitted By: Rayleen Richards, Elections Director
Department: Elections
Motion before the Board:
Approval to conduct Bond Election for the Show Low USD No. 10 on November 7, 2017.
Background:
Elections Office received notice from the Show Low USD NO. 10 to conduct a Special Bond
Election, To  submit to the Qualified Electors thereof the Question of Authorizing the Issuance
and Sale general obligation bonds in the principal amount of not to exceed $10,000,000.00 to
provide money for the following purposes:
Constructing school buildings; Renovating school buildings; Purchasing pupil transportation
vehicles; Acquiring by purchase or lease school lots; Improving school grounds, including
adjacent ways thereto; Supplying school buildings with furniture, equipment and technology;
Liquidating indebtedness incurred for the purposes set forth herein; Providing all utilities and
other capital items necessary for the construction and renovation of school buildings and for
improving school grounds; Paying all architectural, design, engineering, project and construction
management and other costs incurred in connection with the purposes set forth above; and Paying
all legal, financial and other cost in connection with issuance of the bonds.

Board of Supervisors Regular 2.e.        
Meeting Date: 06/27/2017  
Title: CA Aug 2017 Election - Porter Creek DWID
Submitted By: Rayleen Richards, Elections Director
Department: Elections
Motion before the Board:
Resolution Number 11-17, cancelling the August 29, 2017 Election for the Porter Creek
Domestic Water Improvement District and appoint those individuals whom filed
nominating petitions to fill those vacant positions.
Background:
48-1012. Elected board of directors; initial members; qualifications; terms; filling vacancy;
chairperson; reorganization; qualifications of electors
 
E. If only one person files or no person files a nominating petition for election to fill a position on
the board of directors of the district, the county board of supervisors, by resolution, may cancel
the election for that office and appoint the person who filed the nominating petition to fill that
position. If no person files a nominating petition for an election to fill a district board office, the
county board of supervisors, by resolution, may cancel the election for those offices and those
offices are deemed vacant and shall be filled as otherwise provided by law. A person who is
appointed pursuant to this section is fully vested with the powers and duties of the office as if
elected to that office.
 
Attachments
CA DWID Resolution

RESOLUTION NO ______ - 17 
 
A RESOLUTION OF THE NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS, CANCELLING THE ELECTIONS FOR 
THE PORTER CREEK DWID; AND APPOINTING THE CANDIDATE(S) OR DEEMING THE POSITION VACANT, 
PURSUANT TO ARS § 48-1012 (E) 
 
 
 
WHEREAS, ARS § 48-1012 (E) provides as follows with respect to Domestic Water Improvement District 
Board of Directors positions : 
 
“If only one person files or no person files a nominating petition for election to fill a position on the board of 
directors of the district, the county board of supervisors, by resolution, may cancel the election for that office and 
appoint the person who filed the nominating petitio n to fill that position. If no person files a nominating petition for an 
election to fill a district board office, the county board of supervisors, by resolution, may cancel the election for those 
offices and those offices are deemed vacant and shall be filled as otherwise provided by law. A person who is 
appointed pursuant to this section is fully vested with the powers and duties of the office as if elected to that office .” 
 
         and 
 
WHEREAS, A.R.S. § 16-410 (A) authorizes the Board to take such action no earlier than seventy -five (75) 
days before the election, and it is now less than seventy-five (75) days before August 29, 2017 Election at which the 
Domestic Water Improvement Districts Board of Directors will be elected;  
 
         and 
 
WHEREAS, the officer in charge of elections, has certified that the number of persons who filed nominating 
petitions to fill the Domestic Water Improvement Districts Board of Directors positions is less than or equal to the 
number of positions available, attached hereto and incorporated herein as “Exhibit A”;

<!-- page 17 -->
NOW, THEREFORE BE IT RESOLVED by the Board of Supervisors as follows:  
 
That the elections for the Porter Creek DWID Board of Directors positions , attached hereto and incorporated 
herein as “Exhibit A” are hereby cancelled by the Navajo County Board of Supervisors.  
 
That individuals listed as having filed nominating petitions for the Porter Creek DWID Board of Directors 
positions , attached hereto and incorporated herein as “Exhibit A”, are hereby appointed to the position of Board 
Member by the Navajo County Board of Supervisors.  
 
 
 
 
          PASSED, APPROVED AND ADOPTED at Holbrook, Arizona on  June 27, 2017. 
 
  
 NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS 
 
 
                
 By ________________________________________ 
 Jesse Thompson  
 Chairwoman of the Board

Board of Supervisors Regular 2.f.        
Meeting Date: 06/27/2017  
Title: Sale suitcase voting booths to Tribes in Navajo County
Submitted By: Rayleen Richards, Elections Director
Department: Elections
Motion before the Board:
Sale of surplus suitcase style voting booths to Navajo Nation, Hopi Nation and the White
Mountain Apache Tribe for their Tribal Elections for $1.00 each.
Background:
In 2007 Election Services used Grant funds to purchase Pakflatt voting stations, this purchase
brought us into ADA compliance by providing a handicap voting station and eliminated the need
for suitcase style voting booths.

REVOLVING CREDIT AGREEMENT 
 
(NAVAJO COUNTY) 
 
This REVOLVING CREDIT AGREEMENT (the “Agreement”) is made and entered into 
as of July 1, 2017 , by and between NAVAJO COUNTY, Arizona, a county located in, and 
organized and created under the laws of, the State of Arizona, whose mailing address is 100 East 
Code Talkers Drive, South Highway 77, P .O. Box 668, Holbrook, AZ 86025  (hereinafter called 
“County”) and WELLS FARGO BANK, NATIONAL ASSOCIATION, whose mailing address 
is Local Government & Institutional Banking, 100 W. Washington, 13th Floor, Phoenix, Arizona 
85003, (hereinafter called “Bank”).  Bank and County, for and in consideration of the recitals 
and mutual promises contained herein, confirm and agree as follows: 
RECITALS: 
A. Pursuant to Arizona Revised Statutes ( “A.R.S.”) § 11-604.01 (together with all 
amendments and statutes successor thereto, the “Statute”), the County is willing to enter into this 
Agreement with Bank for the purpose of obtaining pursuant to the Statute a revolving line of 
credit to pay the lawful claims and obligations of the County for the current fiscal year that may 
apply for a revolving line of credit hereunder until sufficient monies from Nonrestricted 
Operating Revenues (as defined below) have been received by the County Treasurer (as defined 
below) for payment of the lawful claims and obligations of the County. 
B. Bank is willing to establish a separate and distinct line of credit for the County 
only upon the terms and conditions set forth herein. 
C. The County Treasurer is authorized by the Statute to act as the agent for the 
County and accordingly shall act for the County with respect to its line of credit and any and all 
matters in connection herewith. 
SECTION 1.  DEFINITIONS 
1.1 Definitions.  The following terms, as used herein, shall have the meaning given to 
them in this section or in the numbered section indicated. 
“Advance” means each advance made by Bank to Borrower pursuant to the Credit Line. 
“Borrower” or “County” means Navajo County, Arizona. 
“Borrowing Request ” means a written document substantially in the form attached as 
Exhibit A  hereto and executed by Borrower and acknowledged by the Bank.   
“Business Day ” means any day except a Saturday, Sunday or any other day on which 
commercial banks in Arizona are authorized or required by law to close. 
“Commitment Amount ” means the amount of credit available to Borrower, as such 
amount is determined by Bank in its sole and absolute discretion and memorialized on Schedule

<!-- page 25 -->
2 
4847 -9496-3530.3  
A attached hereto and incorporated herein by this reference, determined by Bank in Bank’s sole 
discretion to be the lesser of (i) the Commitment Amount or (ii) the Statutory Approved Amount. 
“County Treasurer ” means the duly appointed or elected county trea surer of the County 
or its authorized representative. 
“Credit Line” is defined in Paragraph 2.1. 
“Government Auditing Standards ” means those generally accepted accounting principles 
and practices which are recognized as applicable to governmental entities by the Comptroller 
General of the United States. 
“Interest Rate” means (i) [(58)%] Bank’s Prime Rate, or ( ii) the maximum interest rate 
permitted by the Statute for obligations of that type, as described in Paragraph 2.3. 
 “Loan Documents ” means, collectively, this Agreement (including any renewals, 
extensions and modifications thereof), the Services Proposal, any Borrowing Request and any 
written agreements, certificates, letters, declarations or documents executed or delivered to Bank 
pursuant to, or in connection with, this Agreement or the Credit Line. 
“Nonrestricted Operating Revenues ” means all taxes, revenues, earnings or other income 
received by the County  that are not specifically subje ct to restrictions preventing its use for 
repayment of amounts due pursuant to this Agreement. 
“Obligation” means any obligation, including if applicable, this Agreement and the 
Credit Line, the interest on which is included in gross income for purposes of  calculating federal 
income taxes. 
 “Post Maturity Rate ” means the lesser of [four] percent ([4]%) above the rate of interest 
from time to time otherwise applicable to the outstanding principal balance, or the maximum rate 
permitted by the Statute for obligations of that t ype. 
“Prime Rate ” means the rate of interest designated by Bank as its “Prime Rate,” as 
announced by Bank from time to time.  Any interest rate provided for herein that is based upon 
the Prime Rate shall automatically change with, and beco me effective upon, the change in the 
Prime Rate without notice to Borrower. 
“Statute” means Section 11 -604.01, Arizona Revised Statutes as amended, or any 
successor statute for obligations of that type. 
“Statutory Approved Amount ” means the amount shown on Schedule A attached hereto, 
which amount shall be no greater than an amount equal to forty -five percent (45%) of the total 
Nonrestricted Operating Revenues received by Borrower during the immediately preceding fiscal 
year or, if Borrower has not received Nonrestricted Operating Revenues during the immediately 
preceding fiscal year, an amount equal to 45% of Borrower’s estimated first year Nonrestricted 
Operating Revenues.

“Services Proposal ” means the Contract Pursuant to Request for Proposal for Servicing

“Termination Date ” means [June 30, 201 8]. However, Borrower and Bank (acting in its

Termination Date to be extended beyond the final date of the Services Proposal. 
SECTION 2.  THE CREDIT LINE 
2.1 Credit Line; Commitment. 
(a) Subject to the terms and conditions of this Agreement, Bank, from the date 
hereof to, but excluding the Termination Date, shall extend a revolving line of credit to 
Borrower in the Commitment Amount and bearing the Interest Rate as determined by the 
Bank in its sole and absolute discretion, based upon or in reference to r ates established in 
the Services Proposal.  
(b) The revolving line of credit established hereunder (the “Credit Line”) shall 
be initiated by submission of a request (“ Borrowing Request”).  The County Treasurer, 
acting on behalf of Borrower, from time to time, may submit to Bank a Borrowing 
Request.  A Borrowing Request shall include: (i) a Resolution approved by the governing 
body of Borrower, (ii) a designation of the Commitment Amounts, (iii) a designation of 
the Interest Rate , and (i v) be subject to the terms and conditions of this Agreement  and 
the Loan Documents.   
(c) The execution and delivery of this Agreement shall in no wa y require 
Bank to automatically extend the Credit Line  to Borrower , and Bank will look to 
Borrower’s creditworthiness in Bank’s sole determination of whether or not to extend the 
Credit Line to Borrower, at any particular time that a Borrowing Request is delivered to 
the Bank.  
(d) Prior to the Termination Date, Borrower, acting through the County 
Treasurer, may borrow, repay, and reborrow on the Credit Line provided that: (i) no 
Advance shall be made under the Credit Line that would cau se the outstanding principal 
balance of the Credit Line to exceed the Commitment Amount; (ii) no Advance under the 
Credit Line shall be made on or after the Termination Date; and (i ii) no Advance shall be 
made unless all of the conditions precedent set for th in Section 3 below shall have been 
satisfied as of the date the Advance is to be made. 
(e) In no event shall Bank be requir ed to extend to Borrower the Credit Line 
in increments of less than $100,000, without Bank’s prior consent.  
(f) From time to time the individual Commitment Amounts may be increased 
or decreased on the written agreement of the County Treasurer, Borrower, and the Bank 
in accordance with this p aragraph (f).  If Borrower desires to increase or decrease its 
Commitment Amount, the County Treasurer (or its authorized representative thereof) and

<!-- page 27 -->
4 
4847 -9496-3530.3  
Borrower shall complete and sign a Request for Modification of Commitment  Amount 
(“Modification Form”), and deliver the Modification Form to the Bank.  If Bank consents 
to the modification set forth in the Modification Form (as determined by Bank in its sole 
and absolute discretion), Bank shall execute a copy of the Modification Form and return 
same to the County Treasurer.  No increase or decrease of the Commitment Amount shall 
be ef fective until the Bank has received the executed and completed Modification Form 
from the County Treasurer (or its authorized representative) and the Bank shall have 
executed same.  Notwithstanding anything herein to the contrary, the written agreement 
of the County Treasurer and Borrower shall not be required to decrease the Commitment 
Amount of Borrower hereunder following a determination by Bank that an Event of 
Default (as defined below) exists, or that any condition exists that, after notice or the 
passage of time, or both, would constitute an Event of Default. 
2.2 Advances.   
(a) All Advances made by Bank hereunder shall be allocated the Credit Line 
as set forth on Schedule A attached hereto. Periodically the County Treasurer may, from 
time to time, request fo r any such Advance and shall specify the amount of such 
Advance. Any request for such an Advance shall also be accompanied by any items 
required by Sections 2 and 3 of this Agreement.   
(b) A certificate from the County Treasurer certifying that no Advance 
included in the Borrowing Request (i) will cause the outstanding principal balance of the 
Credit Line to exceed the applicable Commitment Amount, (ii) will ca use an Event of 
Default and (iii) Borrower is in compliance with the Statute on the date of the Borrowing 
Request.   
(c) Such other documents or information as Bank may reasonably require. 
(d) In the event that the outstanding principal balance of the Credit Line 
exceeds the applicable Commitment Amount or an Advance included in the Borrowing 
Request would cause the outstanding principal balance of the Credit Line to exceed the 
applicable Commitment Amount, Bank may refuse to make the Advance ; provided  
however that, in any such instance, Borrower shall register any warrants issued in excess 
of its applicable Commitment Amount to the extent and in the manner provided for under 
applicable law 
 
2.3 Interest Rate .  Prior to the occurrence of an Event of Default  (as defined in 
Section 7) under the Credit Line, the outstanding principal balance of the Credit Line shall bear 
interest at the Interest Rate  designated by the Bank.  Upon the occurrence of an Event of Default 
under the Credit Line , the outstanding principal balance of, and all accrued and unpaid interest 
under, the Credit Line shall thereafter bear interest at the Post Maturity Rate.  Interest shall be 
calculated on the basis of a 360 day year, actual days elapsed and shall be due and payable as 
provided in Paragraph 2.5 below.  Bank agrees to use its best efforts to provide the County 
Treasurer notice of a change in  the Prime Rate within two (2) business d ays following such 
change.  Notwithstanding whether timely notice of a change  in Prime Rate is provided by the

Bank, the County Treasurer, on behalf of Borrower, agrees to an effective rate of interest 
hereunder that is the Interest Rate, or if applicable, the Post Maturity Rate as such rate may 
change from time to time.   In no eve nt shall the maximum interest rate on any Commitment 
Amount exceed the maximum rate as provided in the Statute, currently ten percent (10%). 
2.4 [Reserved].   
2.5 Repayments. 
(a) Mandatory Repayments .  If the outstanding principal balance of the 
Credit Line at any time exceed the applicable Commitment Amount, the excess shall be  
immediately due and payable to Bank and Borrower shall be obligated to and the County 
Treasurer shall immediately pay that amount in the manner provided by law. 
(b) Interest Payments .  Interest, at the Interest Rate, on the unpaid principal 
balance of the Credit Line, shall be paid upon receipt of funds from Borrower on a 
quarterly basis beginning September 30, 2017, and on the last day of each December, 
March and June thereafter, and the  balance of all accrued and unpaid interest shall be 
paid on the Termination Date from Nonrestricted Operating Revenues of Borrower. 
In the event Nonrestricted Operating Revenues are insufficient to pay any interest 
when due, the Borrower and the County Tr easurer hereby irrevocably authorize Bank to 
make an Advance from time to time from the Credit Line to pay interest on the Credit 
Line and to pay costs or other charges payable to Bank pursuant to this Agreement 
directly to itself.  Bank shall notify in wr iting the Cou nty Treasurer at least one (1) 
business day prior to any Advance  of the Bank’s payment of such interest or fees.  When 
made by Bank, each such Advance shall be added to the outstanding principal balance of 
the Credit Line and shall be secured by the security interest in the Nonrestricted 
Operating Revenues granted pursuant to this Agreement.  Unless the interest and fees are 
paid from Nonrestricted Operating Revenues, Bank may make an Advance for interest 
payments and fees as and when the same become due, but Bank shall have no obligation 
to make any Advances hereunder and in particular shall have no obligation if: (i) such 
Advance would cause the balance of the Credit Line to exceed the applicable 
Commitment Amount; (ii) any condition then exis ts which would at Bank’s option 
postpone or suspend or terminate Bank’s obligation to make any further Advances; or 
(iii) Bank is then prohibited by law from making an Advance directly to itself for the 
payment of interest or fees (in which event, if permi tted by applicable law, Bank may in 
its discretion make such Advance to Borrower and Borrower shall pay the interest and 
fees therefrom to Bank).  Nothing contained in this Agreement shall be deemed or 
construed to mean that Bank has agreed to permit the p ayment of principal or interest on 
the Credit Line to be due and payable beyond the Termination Date. 
(c) Termination Date Payment .  On the Termination Date, the entire unpaid 
principal balance of each Credit Line, all accrued and unpaid interest on each Credi t Line, 
and all other accrued and unpaid costs, charges and fees as specifically authorized by this 
Agreement, related to each Credit Line shall be due and payable to Bank in full, without 
notice or demand.  Borrower shall be obligated to repay in full on the Termination Date

<!-- page 29 -->
6 
4847 -9496-3530.3  
all such amounts related to its Credit Line.  Any amounts not paid when due shall bear 
interest from its due date until paid at the Post Maturity Rate. 
(d) Optional Prepayments .  Borrower may prepay its Credit Line, in whole or 
in part, at any time or from time to time, without penalty. 
(e) Application of Revenues by County Treasurer .  Pursuant to and in 
accordance with the Statute, the County Treasurer shall first apply immediately upon 
receipt all Nonrestricted Operating Revenues received by Borrower, on behalf of 
Borrower to the repayment of Advances received by Borrower under its Credit Line.  The 
repayment of any Advances shall be made prior to the payment of registered warrants or 
any other obligation, including but not limited to tax anticipation notes, of the Borrower. 
(f) Application of Payments .  All payments received by Bank on the Credit 
Line shall be applied in such order as Bank shall determine, in its sole discretion, to the 
payment of any costs or other charges incurred in connection therewith as specifically 
authorized by the Loan Documents, to the payment of accrued interest, and also to the 
reduction of the principal balance of the Credit Line. 
(g) Place of Payment .  All payments due hereunder shall be made at the 
address for Bank as se t forth above or such other place as Bank may from time to time 
designate in writing. 
2.6 Security Interest, Pledge and Repayment Fund .  Pursuant to the Statute, the 
Bank is entitled to and is hereby granted an irrevocable security interest in the Nonrestricted 
Operating Revenues received by the County Treasurer on behalf of Borrower to the extent of any 
Advances made by Bank to Borrower under its Credit Line. 
All Nonrestricted Operating Revenues of Borrower are hereby irre vocably pledged to 
secure the payment of the Advances, all accrued interest and all costs, fees and other charges 
incurred pursuant to this Agreement.  In the event Nonres tricted Operating Revenues are 
received by or on behalf of Borrower and not immediately paid upon receipt to the Bank the 
County Treasurer shall create a special fund to be known as the Revolving Credit Agreement 
Repayment Fund of the Borrower (the “Repayment Fund ”).  The Repayment Fund shall be 
maintained until such time as moneys in the Repayment Fund are sufficient to pay when due all 
amounts on the Credit Line and until such Credit Line has been repaid in full.  Upon an Advance 
by the Bank to Borrower all Nonrestricted Operating Revenues of the Borrower shall be 
collected and deposited by the County Treasurer into the Repayment Fund of the applicable 
Borrower.  All moneys in the Repayment Fund are pledged to and shall be used solely for the 
payment of the applicable Credit Line 
2.7 Registration of Warrants .  The County Treasurer, as agent for Borrower, shall 
perform its obligations under the Act, including without limitation, A.R.S. § 11 -604.01.E thereof, 
which is hereby i ncorporated herein by reference.  Thi s Paragraph 2.7 shall not obligate Bank to 
register any warrants of Borrower except as provided by law. 
2.8 Credit Line Fees and Expenses.  Borrower agrees to pay, on the closing date, all 
costs and expenses of the Bank in connection with the execution and delivery of this Agreement,

SECTION 3.  CONDITIONS PRECEDENT 
The obligation of Bank to extend the Credit Line to Borrower, and to make each Advance 
thereunder, shall be subject to satisfaction of the following conditions precedent: 
3.1 Closing Documents.  In addition to this Agreement, Bank shall have received for 
that Borrower the following, each in form and substance satisfactory to Bank in its reasonable 
business judgment. 
(a) Financial Information .  The most recent audited financial statements and 
information for that Borrower that is available, including but not limited to , the most 
recent audited and unaudited financial statements, the current year’s budget and “actual 
to budget ” financial reports, and, if requested by Bank,  cash flow projections for that 
Borrower for the fiscal year ending on the Termination Date. 
(b) Borrowing Request .  A fully executed Borrowing Request in a form 
attached as Exhibit A for each Credit Line. 
(c) Opinion from an attorney  for the County in a form substantially in the 
form attached as Exhibit B. 
(d) A resolution from the County approving this Agreement.  
(e) Additional Information .  All other information and documents required to 
be provided to Bank under this Agreement and such other information and documents  as 
Bank may reasonably require. 
3.2 Compliance with Loan Documents .  All agreements, requirements and 
conditions contained in the Loan Documents that are required to be performed or complied, 
including without limitation those contained in Section s 4 and  5 hereof, shall have been 
performed and complied with before or on the date of an Advance under said Borrower’s Credit 
Line. 
3.3 Representations and Warranties .  All representations and warranties contained 
in the Loan Documents shall be true and correct in all respects as of the date of an Advance. 
3.4 No Material Adverse Change .  As of the date of an Advance, no material 
adverse change shall have occurred in the financial condition of that Borrower since the date of

3.5 No Event of Default .  As of the date of an Advance, no Event of default shall 
exist (or result from the making of the Advance), and no event or condit ion shall exist (or result 
from the making of the Advance) that, after notice or lapse of time, or both, would constitute an 
Event of Default.

6.1 Waiver by County Treasurer .  The County Treasurer, on behalf of Borrower,

extension, dishonor, protest, demand and nonpayment, and all other notices and formalities  
except as expressly provided in this Agreement.

Bank shall be construed as a v ariation or waiver of any of the terms, conditions or provisions of

of any other prior or subsequent Event of Default or of the same Event of Default after notice to 
Borrower demanding strict performance. 
SECTION 7.  DEFAULT 
7.1 Events of Default .  The occurrence of any of the following events or conditions 
shall constitute an “Event of Default” with respect to Borrower’s Credit Line: 
(a) Any failure to pay when due any principal, in terest or other amount 
payable pursuant to the Loan Documents with respect to its Credit Line. 
(b) Any failure or neglect in the performance of any condition, requirement, 
provision, term or obligation with respect to Borrower, its Credit Line and/or any 
Advance thereunder, or the Loan Documents. 
(c) Any warranty, representation, or statement with respect to Borrower, its 
Credit Line and/or any Advance thereunder, or the Loan Documents shall be, or shall 
prove to have been, incorrect in any material respect when made or furnished. 
(d) The commencement by Borrower (or against Borrower to which Borrower 
acquiesces or that is not dismissed within forty five (45) days after the filing thereof) of 
any proceeding under the federal bankruptcy laws now or hereafter existing or any other 
similar statute now or hereafter in effect; the entry of any order of relief under such laws;

<!-- page 34 -->

4847 -9496-3530.3  
or the appointment of a receiver, trustee, custodian or conservator of all or any part of the 
assets of Borrower. 
(e) Any of the Loan Documents are terminat ed, declared ineffective, or 
otherwise cease to be legal, valid, and binding agreements, enforceable in accordance 
with the respective terms, so as to deny Bank the benefits contemplated thereby. 
(f) There shall exist or occur any event or condition which Bank  in good faith 
believes impairs, or is substantially likely to impair, the prospect of payment or 
performance by Borrower of its obligations under any of the Loan Documents, including 
without limitation, any event or condition from which Bank reasonably co ncludes 
Borrower is or will be unable to fully repay all Advances to Borrower on or before the 
Termination Date. 
7.2 Remedies Upon an Event of Default .  Upon the occurrence of any Event of 
Default, and at any time thereafter while such Event of Default is cont inuing, Bank may do one 
or more of the following: 
(a) Cease making Advances to Borrower and declare the entire Credit Line 
and all other indebtedness of Borrower under the Loan Documents immediately due and 
payable, without notice or demand. 
(b) Proceed to protect  and enforce its rights and re medies under the Loan 
Documents. 
(c) Avail itself of any other relief to which Bank may be legally or equitably 
entitled. 
7.3 Costs of Enforcement .  All costs and expenses, including without limitation 
court costs and attorneys’ fees, incurred by Bank in enforcing payment and performance of 
Borrower’s Credit Line or in exercising the rights and remedies of Bank in connection therewith 
shall be an additional obligation of Borrower under its Credit Line.

SECTION 9.  MISCELLANEOUS 
9.1 Agreement Survives .  This Agreement shall survive the making of the Credit 
Line and shall continue so long as any part of any Credit Line, or any extension or renewal 
thereof, remains outstanding. 
9.2 Protection of Bank .  All rights, powers and remedies granted Bank herein, or 
otherwise available to Bank, are for the sole benefit and protection of Bank, and Bank may 
exercise any such right, power or remedy at its option and in its sole and absolute discretion 
without any obligation to do so.  In addition, if, under the terms hereof , Bank is given, two or 
more alternative courses of action, Bank may elect any alternative or combination of alternatives, 
at its option and in its sole and absolute discretion.  All monies advanced by Bank and amounts 
paid, suffered or incurred by Bank in  exercising any authority granted herein with respect to the 
Credit Line, including reasonable attorneys’ fees, shall bear interest at the rate payable on the 
Credit Line until paid, and shall be due and payable by Borrower to Bank immediately without 
demand. 
9.3 Time of the Essence.  Time is expressly made of the essence of this Agreement. 
9.4 Notices.  All notices required or permitted to be given hereunder shall be in 
writing and may be given in person or by United States mail, by delivery service or by  electronic 
transmission.  Any notice directed to a party to this Agreement shall become effective upon the 
earliest of the following: (i) actual receipt by that party; (ii) delivery to the designated address of 
that party, addressed to that party; or (iii) if give n by certified or registered United States mail, 
24 hours after deposit with the United States Postal Service, postage prepaid, addressed to that 
party at its designated address.  The designated address of a party shall be the address of that 
party shown at the beginning of this Agreement or such other address as that party, from time to 
time, may specify by notice to the other parties. 
9.5 Governing Law.  This Agreement shall be governed by and construed according 
to the laws of the State of Arizona. 
9.6 Binding Effect.  This Agreement shall be binding upon, and inure to the benefit 
of, the parties hereto and their successors and assigns; provided, however, that without the prior 
written consent of the other party no party may assign any of its rights or delegate a ny of its 
duties hereunder provided.  However, Bank may assign its rights or delegate its duties to an 
entity which directly or indirectly controls, is controlled by, or is under common control with 
Bank.

copies (and by different part ies on different counterpart parties), each of which shall constitute

9.11 Arbitration.  
(a) Arbitration .  The parties hereto agre e, upon demand by any party, to 
submit to binding arbitration all claims, disputes and controversies between or among 
them (and their respective employees, officers, directors, attorneys, and other agents), 
whether in tort, contract or otherwise arising ou t of or relating to in any way (i) the loan 
and related Loan Documents which are the subject of this Agreement and its negotiation, 
execution, collateralization, administration, repayment, modification, extension, 
substitution, formation, inducement, enforcement, default or termination; or (ii) requests 
for additional credit. 
(b) Governing Rules .  Any arbitration proceeding will (i) proceed in a 
location in Arizona selected by the American Arbitration Association ( “AAA”); (ii) be 
governed by the Federal Arbitra tion Act (Title 9 of the United States Code), 
notwithstanding any conflicting choice of law provision in any of the documents between 
the parties; and (iii) be conducted by the AAA, or such other administrator as the parties 
shall mutually agree upon, in a ccordance with the AAA’s commercial dispute resolution 
procedures, unless the claim or counterclaim is at least $1,000,000.00 exclusive of 
claimed interest, arbitration fees and costs in which case the arbitration shall be 
conducted in accordance with the AAA’s optional procedures for large, complex 
commercial disputes (the commercial dispute resolution procedures or the optional 
procedures for large, complex commercial disputes to be referred to, as applicable, as the 
“Rules”).  If there is any inconsistency between the terms hereof and the Rules, the terms 
and procedures set forth herein shall control.  Any party who fails or refuses to submit to 
arbitration following a demand by any other party shall bear all costs and expenses 
incurred by such other part y in compelling arbitration of any dispute.  Nothing contained 
herein shall be deemed to be a waiver by any party that is a bank of the protections 
afforded to it under 12 U.S.C. §91 or any similar applicable state law.

<!-- page 37 -->
14 
4847 -9496-3530.3  
(c) No Waiver of Provisional Remedies, S elf-Help and Injunctive Relief .  
The arbitration requirement does not limit the right of any party to (i) foreclose against 
personal property collateral; (ii) exercise self -help remedies relating to collateral or 
proceeds of collateral such as setoff or re possession; or (iii) obtain provisional or 
ancillary remedies such as replevin, injunctive relief, attachment or the appointment of a 
receiver, before during or after the pendency of any arbitration proceeding.  This 
exclusion does not constitute a waiver of the right or obligation of any party to submit 
any dispute to arbitration or reference hereunder, including those arising from the 
exercise of the actions detailed in subsections (i), (ii) and (iii) of this paragraph. 
(d) Arbitrator Qualifications and Power s.  Any arbitration proceeding in 
which the amount in controversy is $5,000,000.00 or less will be decided by a single 
arbitrator selected according to the Rules, and who shall not render an award of greater 
than $5,000,000.00.  Any dispute in which the am ount in controversy exceeds 
$5,000,000.00 shall be decided by majority vote of a panel of three arbitrators; provided 
however, that all three arbitrators must actively participate in all hearings and 
deliberations.  The arbitrator will be a neutral attorney licensed in the State of Arizona or 
a neutral retired judge of the state or federal judiciary of Arizona, in either case with a 
minimum of ten years’ experience in the substantive law applicable to the subject matter 
of the dispute to be arbitrated.  The arbitrator will determine whether or not an issue is 
arbitratable and will give effect to the statutes of limitation in determining any claim.  In 
any arbitration proceeding the arbitrator will decide (by documents only or with a hearing 
at the arbitrator ’s discretion) any pre -hearing motions which are similar to motions to 
dismiss for failure to state a claim or motions for summary adjudication.  The arbitrator 
shall resolve all disputes in accordance with the substantive law of Arizona and may 
grant any remedy or relief that a court of such state could order or grant within the scope 
hereof and such ancillary relief as is necessary to make effective any award.  The 
arbitrator shall also have the power to award recovery of all costs and fees, to impose 
sanctions and to take such other action as the arbitrator deems necessary to the same 
extent a judge could pursuant to the Federal Rules of Civil Procedure, the Arizona Rules 
of Civil Procedure or other applicable law.  Judgment upon the award rendered by the  
arbitrator may be entered in any court having jurisdiction.  The institution and 
maintenance of an action for judicial relief or pursuit of a provisional or ancillary remedy 
shall not constitute a waiver of the right of any party, including the plaintiff, to submit the 
controversy or claim to arbitration if any other party contests such action for judicial 
relief. 
(e) Discovery.  In any arbitration proceeding discovery will be permitted in 
accordance with the Rules.  All discovery shall be expressly limited to  matters directly 
relevant to the dispute being arbitrated and must be completed no later than 20 days 
before the hearing date and within 180 days of the filing of the dispute with the AAA.   
Any requests for an extension of the discovery periods, or any di scovery disputes, will be 
subject to final determination by the arbitrator upon a showing that the request for 
discovery is essential for the party’s presentation and that no alternative means for 
obtaining information is available.

(f) Class Proceedings and C onsolidations .  The resolution of any dispute 
arising pursuant to the terms of this Agreement shall be determined by a separate 
arbitration proceeding and such dispute shall not be consolidated with other disputes or 
included in any class proceeding. 
(g) Payment Of Arbitration Costs And Fees .  The arbitrator shall award all 
costs and expenses of the arbitration proceeding. 
(h) Miscellaneous.  To the maximum extent practicable, the AAA, the 
arbitrators and the parties shall take all action required to conclude any arbitration 
proceeding within 180 days of the filing of the dispute with the AAA.  No arbitrator or 
other party to an arbitration proceeding may disclose the existence, content or results 
thereof, except for disclosures of information by a party requir ed in the ordinary course 
of its business or by applicable law or regulation.  If more than one agreement for 
arbitration by or between the parties potentially applies to a dispute, the arbitration 
provision most directly related to the Loan Documents or t he subject matter of the dispute 
shall control.  This arbitration provision shall survive termination, amendment or 
expiration of any of the Loan Documents or any relationship between the parties.

shall not be subject to this Section 10.10. 
9.13 Contracted Term of the Servicing Bank .  The term of the period contracted for 
the Bank as servicing bank to the County ( pursuant to A.R.S. Section 35 -325, subsection C) is

Advisor Rules”), agent or a fiduciary of Borrower, (b) Bank is relying on the bank exemption in

REVOLVING CREDIT AGREEMENT 
 
(NAVAJO COUNTY POLITICAL SUBDIVISIONS ) 
 
This REVOLVING CREDIT AGREEMENT (the “Agreement”) is made and entered into 
as of July 1, 2017 , by and between NAVAJO COUNTY, Arizona, a county located in, and 
organized and created under the laws of, the State of Arizona, whose mailing address is 100 East 
Code Talkers Drive, South Highway 77, P.O. Box 6 68, Holbrook, AZ 86025  (hereinafter called 
“County”) and WELLS FARGO BANK,  NATIONAL ASSOCIATION, whose mailing address 
is Local Government & Institutional Banking, 100 W. Washington, 13 th Floor, Phoenix, Arizona 
85003, (hereinafter called “Bank”).  Bank an d County, for and in consideration of the recitals 
and mutual promises contained herein, confirm and agree as follows: 
RECITALS: 
A. Pursuant to Arizona Revised Statutes ( “A.R.S.”) § 11-604.01 (together with all 
amendments and statutes successor thereto, th e “Statute”), the County is willing to enter into this 
Agreement with Bank for the purpose of obtaining pursuant to the Statute a separate and distinct 
revolving line of credit to pay the lawful claims and obligations of each Political Subdivision (as 
defined below) for the current fiscal year that may apply for a revolving line of credit hereunder 
until sufficient monies from Nonrestricted Operating Revenues (as defined below) have been 
received by the County Treasurer (as defined below) for payment of the  lawful claims and 
obligations of the applicable Political Subdivision. 
B. Bank is willing to establish a separate and distinct line of credit for the County 
and each Political Subdivision only upon the terms and conditions set forth herein. 
C. The County Treasurer is authorized by the Statute to act as the agent for each 
Political Subdivision and accordingly shall act for each Political Subdivision with respect to its 
line of credit and any and all matters in connection herewith. 
SECTION 1.  DEFINITIONS 
1.1 Definitions.  The following terms, as used herein, shall have the meaning given to 
them in this section or in the numbered section indicated. 
“Advance” means each advance made by Bank to a Borrower pursuant to that Borrower’s 
Credit Line. 
“Borrower” means jointly any Political Subdivision obtaining a Credit Line under this 
Agreement as set forth on Schedule A attached hereto, as such Schedule A may be revised from 
time to time by the County and the Bank. 
“Borrowing Request ” means a written document substantially in the  form attached as 
Exhibit A hereto and executed by a Borrower and acknowledged by the Bank.   
“Business Day ” means any day except a Saturday, Sunday or any other day on which 
commercial banks in Arizona are authorized or required by law to close.

<!-- page 50 -->
2 
4830 -1435-0154.3  
“Commitment Amount ” means with respect to each Borrower, the amount of credit 
available to such Borrower, as such amount is determined by Bank in its sole and absolute 
discretion and memorialized on Schedule A  attached hereto and incorporated herein by this 
reference, determined by Bank in Bank’s sole discretion to be the lesser of (i) the Commitment 
Amount or (ii) the Statutory Approved Amount. 
“County Treasurer ” means the duly appointed or elected county treasurer of the County  
or its authorized representative. 
“Credit Line” is defined in Paragraph 2.1. 
“Government Auditing Standards ” means those generally accepted accounting principles 
and practices which are recognized as applicable to governmental entities by the Comptroller 
General of the United States. 
“Interest Rate” means (i) [(58)%] of Bank’s Prime Rate, or ( ii) the maximum interest rate 
permitted by the Statute for obligations of that type, as described in Paragraph 2.3. 
 “Loan Documents ” means, collectively,  this Agreement (including any renewals, 
extensions and modifications thereof), the Services Proposal, any Borrowing Request and any 
written agreements, certificates, letters, declarations or documents executed or delivered to Bank 
pursuant to, or in connection with, this Agreement or any Credit Line. 
“Nonrestricted Operating Revenues ” means all taxes, revenues, earnings or other income 
received by a Political Subdivision that are not specifically subject to restrictions preventing their 
use for repayment of amounts due pursuant to this Agreement. 
“Obligation” means any obligation, including if applicable, this Agreement and the 
Credit Line, the interest on which is included in gross income for purposes of calculating federal 
income taxes.

SECTION 2.  THE CREDIT LINES 
2.1 Credit Lines; Commitment. 
(a) Subject to the terms and conditions of this Agreement, Bank, from the date 
hereof to, but excluding the Termination Date, shall extend a separate and distinct 
revolving line of credit to each Borrower in the respective Commitment Amount and 
bearing the Interest Rate as determined by the Bank in its sole and absolute discretion , 
based upon or in reference to rates established in the Services Proposal.  
(b) Each revolving line of credit established hereunder (each a “Credit Line”) 
shall be initiated by submission of a request (“ Borrowing Request”).  The County 
Treasurer, acting on behalf of one or more Borrowers, from time to time, may submit to 
Bank a Borrow ing Request for one or more Credit Lines.  A Borrowing Request shall 
include: (i) a Resolution approved by the governing body of the Borrower, (ii)  a 
designation of the Commitment Amounts, (iii) a designation of the Interest Rate , and (i v) 
be subject to the terms and conditions of this Agreement and the Loan Documents.   
(c) The execution and delivery of this Agreement shall in no wa y require 
Bank to automatically extend a Credit Line to any particular Borrower , and Bank will 
look to each Borrower’s creditworthiness in Bank’s sole determination of whether or not 
to extend a Credit Line to that Borrower, at any particular time that a Borrowing Request 
is delivered to the Bank.  
(d) Prior to the Termination Date, each Borrower, acting through the County 
Treasurer, may borrow, repay, and reborrow on its Credit Line provided that: (i) no 
Advance shall be made under a Credit Line that would cause the outstanding principal 
balance of that Credit Line to exceed the Commitment Amount for that Borrower; (ii) no 
Advance shall be made that would cause the aggregate outstanding principal balance of 
all Credit Lines to exceed the Total Commitment; (iii) no Advance under a Credit Line 
shall be made on or after the Termination Date; and (iv) no Advance shall be made unless

all of the conditions precedent set forth in Section 3 below shall have been satisfied as to 
that Credit Line as of the date the Advance is to be made. 
(e) The aggregate outstanding principal balances of all Credit Lines shall not 
exceed, at any time, an amo unt equal to the Total Commitment.  Notwithstanding that the 
cumulative total of the Commitment Amounts of each Borrower is greater than the Total 
Commitment, Bank shall have no obligation to make any Advance under any Credit Line 
which would cause the tot al outstanding principal balance to all Borrowers to exceed the 
Total Commitment. 
(f) The Total Commitment may only be increased hereunder after (i) receipt 
by Bank from the County Treasurer of a written request to do so together with 
information as to which Credit Lines are to be increased, and (ii) delivery by Bank to the 
County Treasurer of its consent to do so. 
(g) In no event shall Bank be requir ed to extend to any particular Borrower a 
Credit Line of less than $100,000, without Bank’s prior consent.  
(h) From time to time the individual Commitment Amounts may be increased 
or decreased on the written agreement of the County Treasurer, the Borrower, and the 
Bank in accordance with this paragraph (h).  If a Borrower desires to increase or decrease 
its respective Comm itment Amount, the County Treasurer (or its authorized 
representative thereof) and the Borrower shall complete and sign a Request for 
Modification of Commitment  Amount ( “Modification Form ”), and deliver the 
Modification Form to the Bank.  If Bank consents to the modification set forth in the 
Modification Form (as determined by Bank in its sole and absolute discretion), Bank 
shall execute a copy of the Modification Form and return same to the County Treasurer.  
No increase or decrease of any Commitment Amoun t shall be effective until the Bank has 
received the executed and completed Modification Form from the County Treasurer (or 
its authorized representative) and the Bank shall have executed same.  No increased 
Commitment Amount shall, of itself, cause a comm ensurate increase in the Total 
Commitment, unless expressly consented to in writing by the Bank.  Notwithstanding 
anything herein to the contrary, the written agreement of the County Treasurer and any 
Borrower shall not be required to decrease the Commitmen t Amount of any Borrower 
hereunder following a determination by Bank that an Event of Default (as defined below) 
exists with respect to such Borrower, or that any condition exists that, after notice or the

Borrower.

2.3 Interest Rate .  Prior to the occurrence of an Event of Default (as defined in 
Section 7) under a Credit Line, the outstanding principal balance of a Credit L ine shall bear 
interest at the Interest Rate  designated by the Bank.  Upon the occurrence of an Event of Default 
under a Credit Line, the outstanding principal balance of, and all accrued and unpaid interest 
under, a Credit Line shall thereafter bear inter est at the Post Maturity Rate.  Interest shall be 
calculated on the basis of a 360 day year, actual days elapsed and shall be due and payable as 
provided in Paragraph 2.5 below.  Bank agrees to use its best efforts to provide the County 
Treasurer notice of  a change in  the Prime Rate within two (2) business d ays following such 
change.  Notwithstanding whether timely notice of a change in Prime Rate is provided by the

hereunder that is the Interest Rate, or if applicable, the Post Maturity Rate as such rate may 
change from time to time.   In no event shall the maximum interest rate on any Commitment 
Amount exceed the maximum rate as provided in the Statute, currently ten percent (10%). 
2.4 [Reserved].   
2.5 Repayments.

(a) Mandatory Repayments .  If the outstanding principal balance of a Credit 
Line at any time exceed the applicable Commitment Amount, the excess shall be  
immediately due and payable to Bank and the Borrower shall be obligated to and the 
County Treasurer shall immediately pay that amount in the manner provided by law. 
(b) Interest Payments .  Interest, at the Interest Rate, on the unpaid principal 
balance of each Credit Line, shall be paid upon receipt of funds from the Borrower on a 
quarterly basis beginning September 30, 2017, and on the last day of each December, 
March and June thereafter , and the balance of all accrued and unpaid interest shall be 
paid on the Termination Date from Nonrestricted Operating Revenues of the Borrower. 
In the event Nonrestricted Operating Revenues are insufficient to pay any interest 
when due, the Borrower and the County Treasurer hereby irrevocably authorize Bank to 
make an Advance from time to time from the Credit Line to pay interest on t he Credit 
Line and to pay costs or other charges payable to Bank pursuant to this Agreement 
directly to itself.  Bank shall notify in writing the Cou nty Treasurer at least one (1) 
business day prior to any Advance  of the Bank’s payment of such interest or fees.  When

payments and fees as and when the same become due, but Bank shall have no obligation 
to make any Advances hereunder and in particular shall have no obligation if: (i) such 
Advance would cause the balance of the Credit Line to exceed the applicable 
Commitment Amount; (ii) any condition then exists which would at Bank’s option 
postpone or suspend or terminate Bank’s obligation to make any further Advances; or 
(iii) Bank is then prohibited by law from making an Advance directly to itself for the 
payment of interest or fees (in which event, if permitted by applicable law, Bank may in 
its discretion make such Advance to Borrower and Borrower shall pay the interest and  
fees therefrom to Bank).  Nothing contained in this Agreement shall be deemed or 
construed to mean that Bank has agreed to permit the payment of principal or interest on 
the Credit Line to be due and payable beyond the Termination Date. 
(c) Termination Date P ayment.  On the Termination Date, the entire unpaid

and all other accrued and unpaid costs, charges and fees as specifically authorized by this

notice or demand.  Each Borrower shall be obligated to repay in full on the Termination 
Date all such amounts related to its Credit Line.  Any amounts not paid when due shall 
bear interest from its due date until paid at the Post Maturity Rate.  
(d) Optional Prepayments .  Each Borrower may prepay its Credit Line, in 
whole or in part, at any time or from time to time, without penalty.

Bank is entitled to and is hereby granted an irrevocable security interest in the Nonrestricted 
Operating Revenues received by the County Treasurer on behalf of each Borrower to the extent 
of any Advances made by Bank to a Borrower under its Credit Line. 
All Nonrestricted Operating Revenues of any Borrower are hereby irre vocably pledged to 
secure the payment of the Advances, all accrued interest and all costs, fees and other charges 
incurred pursuant to this Agreement.  In the event Nonrestricted Operating Revenues are 
received by or on behalf of a Borrower and not immediately paid upon receipt to the Bank the 
County Treasurer shall create a special fund to be known as the Revolving Credit Ag reement 
Repayment Fund of the Borrower (the “Repayment Fund”).  The Repayment Fund shall be 
maintained until such time as moneys in the Repayment Fund are sufficient to pay when due all 
amounts on the Credit Line and until such Credit Line has been repaid in full.  Upon an Advance 
by the Bank to a Borrower all Nonrestricted Operating Revenues of the Borrower shall be 
collected and deposited by the County Treasurer into the Repayment Fund of the applicable 
Borrower.  All moneys in the Repayment Fund are pled ged to and shall be used solely for the 
payment of the applicable Credit Line.

with respect to any Borrower shall constitute an “Event of Default ” with respect to that 
Borrower’s Credit Line: 
(a) Any failure to pay when due any principal, interest or other amount 
payable pursuant to the Loan Documents with respect to its Credit Line. 
(b) Any failure or neglect in the performance of any condition, requirement, 
provision, term or obligation with respect to B orrower, its Credit Line and/or any

(c) Any warranty, representation, or statement with respect to Borrower, its

prove to have been, incorrect in any material respect when made or furnished.

acquiesces or that is not dismissed within forty five (45) days after the filing thereof) of 
any proceeding under the federal bank ruptcy laws now or hereafter existing or any other 
similar statute now or hereafter in effect; the entry of any order of relief under such laws 
with respect to Borrower; or the appointment of a receiver, trustee, custodian or 
conservator of all or any part of the assets of Borrower.

that party, addressed to that pa rty; or (iii) if given by certified or registered United States mail, 
24 hours after deposit with the United States Postal Service, postage prepaid, addressed to that 
party at its designated address.  The designated address of a party shall be the address of that 
party shown at the beginning of this Agreement or such other address as that party, from time to 
time, may specify by notice to the other parties. 
9.5 Governing Law.  This Agreement shall be governed by and construed according 
to the laws of the State of Arizona. 
9.6 Binding Effect.  This Agreement shall be binding upon, and inure to the benefit 
of, the parties hereto and their successors and assigns; provided, however, that without the prior 
written consent of the other party no party may assign any of its  rights or delegate any of its 
duties hereunder provided.  However, Bank may assign its rights or delegate its duties to an 
entity which directly or indirectly controls, is controlled by, or is under common control with 
Bank. 
9.7 Headings.  The headings or captions of sections in this Agreement are for 
reference only, do not define or limit the provisions of such sections, and shall not affect the 
interpretation of this Agreement.

copies (and by different parties on different counterpart parties), each of which shall constitute 
an original but such copies shall constitute the same agreement , either in original, copy, or 
portable document format (“pdf”) format.

9.11 Arbitration.  
(a) Arbitration .  The parties hereto agree, upon demand by any party, to 
submit to binding arbitration all claims, disputes and controversies between or among 
them (and their res pective employees, officers, directors, attorneys, and other agents), 
whether in tort, contract or otherwise arising out of or relating to in any way (i) the loan 
and related Loan Documents which are the subject of this Agreement and its negotiation, 
execution, collateralization, administration, repayment, modification, extension, 
substitution, formation, inducement, enforcement, default or termination; or (ii) requests 
for additional credit. 
(b) Governing Rules .  Any arbitration proceeding will (i) proceed in a 
location in Arizona selected by the American Arbitration Association ( “AAA”); (ii) be 
governed by the Federal Arbitration Act (Title 9 of the United States Code), 
notwithstanding any conflicting choice of law provision in any of the documents between 
the parties; and (iii) be conducted by the AAA, or such other administrator as the parties 
shall mutually agree upon, in accordance with the AAA’s commercial dispute resolution 
procedures, unless the claim or counterclaim is at least $1,000,000.00 exclusive o f 
claimed interest, arbitration fees and costs in which case the arbitration shall be 
conducted in accordance with the AAA’s optional procedures for large, complex 
commercial disputes (the commercial dispute resolution procedures or the optional 
procedures for large, complex commercial disputes to be referred to, as applicable, as the 
“Rules”).  If there is any inconsistency between the terms hereof and the Rules, the terms 
and procedures set forth herein shall control.  Any party who fails or refuses to su bmit to 
arbitration following a demand by any other party shall bear all costs and expenses 
incurred by such other party in compelling arbitration of any dispute.  Nothing contained 
herein shall be deemed to be a waiver by any party that is a bank of the p rotections 
afforded to it under 12 U.S.C. §91 or any similar applicable state law. 
(c) No Waiver of Provisional Remedies, Self -Help and Injunctive Relief .  
The arbitration requirement does not limit the right of any party to (i) foreclose against 
personal property collateral; (ii) exercise self -help remedies relating to collateral or 
proceeds of collateral such as setoff or repossession; or (iii) obtain provi sional or

<!-- page 63 -->
15 
4830 -1435-0154.3  
ancillary remedies such as replevin, injunctive relief, attachment or the appointment of a 
receiver, before during or after the pendency of any arbitration proceeding.  This 
exclusion does not constitute a waiver of the right or obligation of any  party to submit 
any dispute to arbitration or reference hereunder, including those arising from the 
exercise of the actions detailed in subsections (i), (ii) and (iii) of this paragraph. 
(d) Arbitrator Qualifications and Powers .  Any arbitration proceeding in 
which the amount in controversy is $5,000,000.00 or less will be decided by a single 
arbitrator selected according to the Rules, and who shall not render an award of greater 
than $5,000,000.00.  Any dispute in which the amount in controversy exceeds 
$5,000,000.00 shall be decided by majority vote of a panel of three arbitrators; provided 
however, that all three arbitrators must actively participate in all hearings and 
deliberations.  The arbitrator will be a neutral attorney licensed in the State of Arizon a or 
a neutral retired judge of the state or federal judiciary of Arizona, in either case with a 
minimum of ten years’ experience in the substantive law applicable to the subject matter 
of the dispute to be arbitrated.  The arbitrator will determine whether or not an issue is 
arbitratable and will give effect to the statutes of limitation in determining any claim.  In 
any arbitration proceeding the arbitrator will decide (by documents only or with a hearing 
at the arbitrator’s discretion) any pre -hearing motions which are similar to motions to 
dismiss for failure to state a claim or motions for summary adjudication.  The arbitrator 
shall resolve all disputes in accordance with the substantive law of Arizona and may 
grant any remedy or relief that a court of such state could order or grant within the scope 
hereof and such ancillary relief as is necessary to make effective any award.  The 
arbitrator shall also have the power to award recovery of all costs and fees, to impose 
sanctions and to take such other act ion as the arbitrator deems necessary to the same 
extent a judge could pursuant to the Federal Rules of Civil Procedure, the Arizona Rules 
of Civil Procedure or other applicable law.  Judgment upon the award rendered by the 
arbitrator may be entered in any  court having jurisdiction.  The institution and 
maintenance of an action for judicial relief or pursuit of a provisional or ancillary remedy 
shall not constitute a waiver of the right of any party, including the plaintiff, to submit the 
controversy or cla im to arbitration if any other party contests such action for judicial 
relief. 
(e) Discovery.  In any arbitration proceeding discovery will be permitted in 
accordance with the Rules.  All discovery shall be expressly limited to matters directly 
relevant to the  dispute being arbitrated and must be completed no later than 20 days 
before the hearing date and within 180 days of the filing of the dispute with the AAA.   
Any requests for an extension of the discovery periods, or any discovery disputes, will be 
subject to final determination by the arbitrator upon a showing that the request for 
discovery is essential for the party’s presentation and that no alternative means for 
obtaining information is available. 
(f) Class Proceedings and Consolidations .  The resolution of any dispute 
arising pursuant to the terms of this Agreement shall be determined by a separate 
arbitration proceeding and such dispute shall not be consolidated with other disputes or 
included in any class proceeding.

<!-- page 64 -->
16 
4830 -1435-0154.3  
(g) Payment Of Arbitration Costs And Fees .  The arbitrator shall award all 
costs and expenses of the arbitration proceeding. 
(h) Miscellaneous.  To the maximum extent practicable, the AAA, the 
arbitrators and the parties shall take all action required to conclude any arbitration 
proceeding within 180 days of the filing of the dispute with the AAA.  No arbitrator or 
other party to an arbitration proceeding may disclose the existence, content or results 
thereof, except for disclosures of information by a party required in the ordinary course 
of its busin ess or by applicable law or regulation.  If more than one agreement for 
arbitration by or between the parties potentially applies to a dispute, the arbitration 
provision most directly related to the Loan Documents or the subject matter of the dispute 
shall control.  This arbitration provision shall survive termination, amendment or 
expiration of any of the Loan Documents or any relationship between the parties.

involved in initiating, negotiating, securing, drafting or creating the contract on behalf of the 
State of Arizona, its political subdivisions or any department or agency of either from any other 
party to the contract arising as the result of the contract.  Any Borrower described in A.R.S. 
Section 38-511.G, as amended, as not included within the definition of “political subdivision” for 
purposes of A.R.S. Section 38-511 shall not be subject to this Section 10.10. 
9.13 Contracted Term of the Servicing Bank .  The term of the period contracted for 
the Bank as servicing bank to the County ( pursuant to A.R.S. Section 35 -325, subsection C) is 
the term described in the Services Proposal. 
9.14 Arm’s Length Transaction.  The County and each Borrower acknowledges and

15B of the Securities and Exchange Act of 1934, as amended, and the related final rules (the 
“Municipal Advisor Rules”), agent or a fiduciary of any Borrower, (b) Bank is relying o n the

Board of Supervisors Regular 2.h.        
Meeting Date: 06/27/2017  
Title: ACJC Byrne Grant DC-18-009
Submitted For: KC Clark, Sheriff  Submitted By: Cheryl Powell, Deputy Chief 
Department: Sheriff 
Motion before the Board:
Arizona Criminal Justice Commission Byrne grant #DC-18-009 for $234,416 for operation of the
Major Crimes Apprehension Team.
Background:
The Sheriff's Office has once again been fortunate to receive funding from the Arizona Criminal
Justice Commission for funding under the Drug, Gang and Violent Crime Control Program.  The
funding pays for partial salaries and employee related expenses for operation of the Major Crimes
Apprehension Team (MCAT).  The funds pay for one deputy from the Sheriff's Office and one
officer each from the Cities of Winslow and Show Low and the Towns of Pinetop-Lakeside and
Snowflake-Taylor.
A match is required with this cycle - $58,604 and will be expensed from one of the RICO funds. 
The award period is 7-01-17 thru 6-30-18

This Grant Agreement is made this 2 nd day of June, 2017 by and between the ARIZONA CRIMINAL 
JUSTICE COMMISSION hereinafter called “COMMISSION” and NAVAJO COUNTY , through Navajo

NOW, THEREFORE, it is agreed between the parties as follows: 
 
1. This Agreement will commence on July 1, 2017 and terminate on June 30, 20 18.  This 
Agreement expires at the end of the award period unless prior written approval for an 
extension has been obtained from the COMMISSION.  A request for an extension must be 
received by the COMMISSION sixty (60) days prior to the end of the award pe riod.  The 
COMMISSION in its sole discretion may approve an extension that furthers the goals and 
objectives of the program and shall determine the length of any extension. 
 
2. GRANTEE agrees that grant funds will be used in accordance with applicable program  rules, 
guidelines and special conditions.

standards and those outlined in the grant application.  Substandard performance as 
determined by the COMMISSION will constitut e noncompliance with this Agreement.  If the 
COMMISSION finds noncompliance, the GRANTEE will receive a written notice which identifies 
the area of noncompliance, and the appropriate corrective action to be taken.  If the GRANTEE 
does not respond within thirty (30) calendar days to this notice, and does not provide sufficient 
information concerning the steps which are being taken to correct the problem, the 
COMMISSION may suspend funding, permanently terminate this Agreement or revoke the 
grant.  
 
4. Any devia tion or failure to comply with the purpose and/or conditions of this Agreement 
without prior written COMMISSION approval may constitute sufficient reason for the 
COMMISSION to terminate this Agreement, revoke the grant, require the return of all unspent 
funds, perform an audit of expended funds, and require the return of any previously spent 
funds which are deemed to have been spent in violation of the purpose or conditions of this 
grant.

Director or by persons authorized by the Executive Director on behalf of the COMMISSION and
